![](https://i.ormos.uk/3b/82/3b825acfd7f4c31ec4d837e39c9fb7f844c8c5010e208b14b7109cc0e5e9ffd6.jpg)

The German Nationals 2006 are over, and just like last year, it's Ninjas at the top! Maximilian Bracht, Germany's rising star, has taken top honors. His new Erayo-Ninja deck proved to be strong, although Bracht claims that his worst matchup is Solar Flare - the most played deck at this Nationals, and that he had good luck in the Swiss. Joining him on the National team are Frenchman Amiel Tenenbaum and Jörg Unfried, who defeated Tobias Henke in the playoff for third place. They made their way through a very strong top 8, which also featured Holger Althues, who made his third Nationals top 8 at this event.

The whole event showed off German Magic at its best. 158 Nationals competitors, 137 Legacy competitors, close to 40 JSS competitors and the best the community has to offer made the whole weekend a great one. The players party on Saturday night offered respite from the Magical cards, but like players all over the world, even then some Magic enthusiasts couldn't keep away from their decks to enjoy bowling and pool billiard!

I hope you enjoyed the coverage here and over at [PlanetMTG.de](http://www.planetmtg.de/events/coverage.html?id=19) . Now that the tournament is over, I can finally go celebrate my birthday! You can return to magicthegathering.com for more coverage of premier tournaments around the world next weekend for GP Hiroshima and the week after that for Japanese Nationals. So long and auf Wiedersehen from Aschaffenburg!

## Sunday, August 13: 11:40 a.m. - Tired after the players party

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e5/4e/e54eba994c0ad6afb7e5ea50997f1c6084383de06f21fb1c934a8e1f94ef7022.jpg)*Judge Jens Strohäcker loves the Dreamblade minis.*

Yesterday, the players party in a local bowling hall united all competitors for a night of relaxation and fun at the pool tables! With bowling records stretching all the way from below 30 to close to 200, the competition took a back seat and watched the fun let its hair down.

Top 8 competitors Holger Althues and Tobi Henke weren't in for bowling, though. They both wanted to test their matchups for this morning, so they figured they might as well play against each other! They played a best-of-five match which Holger's [Enduring Ideal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enduring+Ideal) won handily against Henke's red-white burn deck. Tobias Henke was a little dejected, because he had figured that his deck would stand at least an even chance.

Having lost that test match, Henke, who is the editor of a major German strategy site, chose to test his skill at pool instead, challenging the editors of the other two major Magic strategy and community sites. Apparently he had better matchups there, because he dispatched me (standing in for PlanetMTG.de) in a close match and Klaus-Michael "EvilBernd" Bredt in short order.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/95/a9/95a95cfd8fbe29ada397c786e6b0b2f89095025f0a0b49b913860ccce519c480.jpg)*Tobias Henke, left, sunk his balls faster than Klaus-Michael 'EvilBernd' Bredt.*

Those players who didn't want to play Magic, pool billiard or shoot some bowling balls down the lanes resorted to poker instead. Another popular game especially among the Wizards people and the judges was Dreamblade, the new miniature game. The detailed figures attracted the attention of a couple of spectators, because they do, in fact, look nice (especially that small dwarf with the big axe). When one o'clock in the night was through, the party was called to an end.

"It was real funny, really!" commented Harald Stein, who had a laughing fit during the party. Together with Reinhard Blech and David Brucker, something they wouldn't tell had the three of them in pieces, laughing. Even without the complimentary beer that many players had wished for, the party just can't have been bad if so much fun was had!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/b4/8e/b48e9e2654e1a78e1a9d1419790e0e2eb798a7b306680ffea46b5ae211268d1b.jpg)*Sending those balls rolling was almost as fun as turning the cards sideways.*  

## Sunday, August 13: 1:37 p.m. - The quarterfinals summary in a language you can understand

I can't split myself in four. I wish I could, because then I could bring full feature matches from all the quarterfinal tables to you. But I can't, so you will get the complete German feature matches here as well as on PlanetMTG. What I will do is to provide English language summaries of the quarters and semis, until the final comes along which I will cover.

**Quarterfinal 1**

Quarterfinal one saw Frenchman Amiel Tenenbaum deliver serious beats to Rosario Maji, taking out the Dutch-German 3-0. Rosario's Solar Flare build had no chance against the Frenchman's [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) deck. In game 1, Amiel's land destruction kept Rosario off black mana, and Boseiju forced through a [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire) that devastated Rosario's land base. A 10/10 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) finished Rosario off. Game 2 saw more of the same. Even though Rosario resolved [Persecute](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Persecute), catching a [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire) and two [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain)s, Amiel topdecked a 5/5 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) and followed it up with a second. Rosario named Black Lotus for his [Cranial Extraction](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cranial+Extraction) to see Amiel's deck, but still lost Game 2. Game 3 kicked off with [Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish) and [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) from Amiel. Rosario had not much more than an early [Castigate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Castigate) and a [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn), but Amiel [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)ed the [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn) to keep his [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore), and that was that.

Amiel Tenenbaum 3 - 0 Rosario Maji

**Quarterfinal 2**

Quarterfinal two featured Jörg Unfried with Hand to Hand against Harald Stein with Zoo. Harald is already qualified for Worlds over rating, so there is nothing on the line for him, and Unfried is a friend of his. The matches were fast, with Jörg losing game 1 after mulliganing down to four cards. Game 2 was decided by Ghost Council and [Dark Confidant](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dark+Confidant), revealing a timely [Shining Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shining+Shoal) for Harald and Jörg had to pack up his cards. Game 3 saw Harald going down to six cards, but manages to disenchant a [Pacifism](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pacifism) with a crucial [Mortify](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mortify). Despite being in an advantageous situation, Harald concedes to Jörg and hopes that he will join him on the trip to Worlds in November.

Jörg Unfried 3 - 2 Harald Stein

**Quarterfinal 3**

Tobias Henke with his red-white burn deck defeated Holger Althues with Endurind Ideal in the third quarterfinal. They went to five games, with Holger winning the games where he could use his [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex)es to keep Tobias away from enough mana to burn him out. [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) also held off the creature beats and helped looking for answers to Tobias' [Lightning Helix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Helix)es and [Char](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Char)s. That's how the first four games played out, but the fifth one was pretty exciting: Holger had Vitu-Ghazi cranking out tokens and was at ten life, with Tobias at twelve. [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) and [Flames of the Blood Hand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flames+of+the+Blood+Hand) took Holger to three life, while his tokens and a [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) threatened lethal damage. Tobias ripped a [Char](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Char) from the top of his library to take a very close Game 5 and the match.

Holger Althues 2 - 3 Tobias Henke

**Quarterfinal 4**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/22/37/22376b5156a79bfea5e82b50af2233107ce3187b6eaed8ca500d0ea8c5071bbc.jpg)*The four semi-finalists who will take the real trip to Paris: Maximilian Bracht, Tobias Henke, Jörg Unfried and Amiel Tenenbaum (from left).*

The last quarterfinal took ages to finish. Maximilian Bracht with his Erayo-Ninja deck went to five games against Carlo Mazzurco's Snakes. It was a match where zero-power creatures from Maximilian Bracht, [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter)s and [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), stood against one-power snakes from Carlo, who couldn't kill Bracht's blockers. Both players had [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) in their decks, but Maximilian definitely had the Ninja advantage. He lost game 1 to [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte), which is a crucial card in this matchup for both decks. Game 2 had the reverse situation: Bracht ripped two Jittes to overcome Carlo's sideboarded [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare), and Carlo's own Jitte came too late to save him. In game 3, Carlo did a lot of damage with [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper), but Bracht had Jitte again and was able to stop Carlo's own pointy fork with [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal). [Sosuke's Summons](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sosuke%27s+Summons) featured heavily in game 4, where Carlo used [Sundering Vitae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sundering+Vitae) to keep Maximilian's Jittes off the table. In game 5, Carlo conceded to Maximilian with an undecided board position, because he knew he was very unlikely to be able to attend Worlds.

Maximilian Bracht 3 - 2 Carlo Mazzurco

  

## Sunday, August 13: 1:52 p.m. - Quarterfinals: Rosario Maij (Solar Flare) vs. Amiel Tenenbaum (Vore)

### Game 1

Statt einem Würfelwurf entscheiden sich die beiden, das Alter des Judges als Kriterium zu nehmen, wer beginnt (Amiel gewinnt). Amiel beginnt ganz gut mit [Eye of Nowhere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Nowhere) auf eine [Hallowed Fountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hallowed+Fountain), hat dann aber als nächstes Play erst wieder ein [Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish), welches remanded wird. Rosario kann sein Mana mit einem Signet aufbauen und versucht ein [Zombify](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Zombify) auf den [Angel of Despair](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Angel+of+Despair), den er zuvor discarden musste, aber ein Leak stoppt diesen Plan. [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) für Rosario und Research für Amiel verbessern jeweils die Hände, Amiel kann nun aber den [Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish) resolven auf [Caves of Koilos](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Caves+of+Koilos), was Rosario erneut von schwarzem Mana abschneidet.

Amiel benutzt sein Boseiju, um ein [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire) durchzuforcen und hat nun 5 Mana gegenüber einem Signet bei Rosario, der noch drei Leben bekommt, indem er den [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) in Miren opfert.  
 Mehrere Landzerstörer sorgen dafür, dass es auch bei der Manaknappheit bleibt. Ein 10/10 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) betritt das Spiel für Rosarios [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) hat Amiel noch einen [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Drei Sorceries und einen Zug später ist das erste Spiel Geschichte.

Rosario Maji 0 - 1 Amiel Tenenbaum

SB:  
 Rosario nimmt die Wraths, zwei [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn)s, zwei Angel, einen Kokusho und einen [Zombify](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Zombify) raus, und boarded [Descendant of Kiyomaro](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Descendant+of+Kiyomaro), Extraction, [Persecute](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Persecute) und [Castigate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Castigate). Amiel nimmt 2 [Smash](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Smash)es, 3 [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand)s, Boseiju und Meloku ins Deck, und boarded 4 [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm), ein [Tidings](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tidings), einen [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) und einen [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) raus.

### Game 2

Amiel kann ein Signet bouncen, aber Rosario baut schnell wieder auf und kann mit [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand)-Backup ein [Persecute](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Persecute) gegen einen [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) durchforcen. Amiel discarded [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire) und zwei [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) (er hält noch [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) und zwei Länder), topdecked aber dann einen [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) (5/5 zu diesem Zeitpunkt).

Rosario spielt einen [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar), für einen zweiten opfert Amiel mit [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) und [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) gleich zwei Counter. Der Grund wird klar, als er einen zweiten [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) spielt. Nachdem Rosario noch eine Extraction auf Black Lotus gespielt hat, um sich Amiels Deck anzusehen, stirbt er beim nächsten Angriff.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/5e/3a/5e3a5590ca6795183c70f8b70669d2896cec8e58a318e28c5add3a471b525b86.jpg)*Rosario Maji (left) and Amiel Tenenbaum wait for their match to start.*

Rosario Maji 0 - 2 Amiel Tenenbaum

### Game 3

Rosario startet wieder mit einem Signet. Amiel hat diesmal keinen Bounce, aber Rosario kein drittes Land und auch sein nächster Draw liefert ihm keines. Er versucht ein [Compulsive Research](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Compulsive+Research), welches aber remanded wird, was Amiel erlaubt, gefahrlos ein [Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish) zu spielen.

Ein [Castigate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Castigate) von Rosario zeigt Doppel-Research, [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain), [Eye of Nowhere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Nowhere) und [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) auf Amiels Hand. Rosario nimmt ein Research, aber [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) wirft ihn auf ein Land zurück. [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) betritt als 4/4 das Spiel und ein [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn) von Rosario fängt sich einen Manaleak. Rosario kann nichts mehr machen angesichts von Amiels Hand und gibt auf.

Rosario Maji 0 - 3 Amiel Tenenbaum

  

## Sunday, August 13: 2:05 p.m. - Quarterfinals: Harald Stein (Zoo) vs. Jörg Unfried (Hand to Hand)

Harald und Jörg kennen sich schon aus gemeinsamen Testsessions. So ist es nicht verwunderlich, dass die Stimmung zwischen den beiden durchaus freundlich ist. Außerdem hat Jörg gegen Harald Runde 14 aufgegeben, und ihm so den Platz in den Top 8 gesichert. Harald ist über Rating schon für die WM qualifiziert, so dass vor Beginn des Matches schon klar ist, wer die höhere Motivation zu gewinnen hat.

### Game 1

![otherworldly Journey](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=otherworldly+Journey)

Jörg gewinnt den Würfelwurf und beginnt mit einer virtuellen Reise nach Paris. Die obersten Karten seiner Bibliothek rechtfertigen seine Entscheidung: Die nächsten sechs Karten revealen zwei [Volcanic Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Hammer), Mauler, und drei Länder. Auch diese Hand kann Jörg nicht halten. Der [Scab-Clan Mauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scab-Clan+Mauler) auf der fünf Karten Hand ohne weitere Kreatur ist wieder Anlass zur bitteren Pille, die eine Vier-Karten-Starthand bedeutet.

Der [Kird Ape](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kird+Ape) 1st Turn ist diesmal ein eine passable Eröffnung, allerdings mit einer [Sacred Foundry](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sacred+Foundry). [Hand of Honor](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hand+of+Honor), [Paladin en-Vec](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Paladin+en-Vec) und dann Jitte für Harald sorgen für eine Turn 4 Aufgabe von Jörg.

Sideboard-Entscheidungen:  
 3 [Castigate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Castigate), 3 8-1/2 Tails, 1 [Dark Confidant](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dark+Confidant), 1 [Cranial Extraction](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cranial+Extraction), 1 Ghost- Council gehen bei Harald raus. 3 Descendant of Kiyamaro, 3 Empty Shrine Kannushi und 3 [Last Gasp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Last+Gasp) kommen rein.  
 4 [Dryad Sophisticate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dryad+Sophisticate), 4 [Isamaru, Hound of Konda](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Isamaru%2C+Hound+of+Konda), 1 Mauler und 1 [Savannah Lions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Savannah+Lions) verlassen Haralds Zoo. Dafür kommen 2 [Otherworldly Journey](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Otherworldly+Journey), 2 Tin-Street Hooligan, 3 [Pacifism](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pacifism) und 3 Jitte

Zeilen geschrieben über Mulligan- und Sideboardentscheidungen : 9  
 Zeilen geschrieben über das Spiel: 2

Harald Stein 1 - 0 Jörg Unfried

### Game 2

Drei Affen und ein [Stomping Ground](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stomping+Ground) auf der Hand versprechen einen besseren Start bei Jörg. Der [Empty-Shrine Kannushi](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Empty-Shrine+Kannushi) aufs Haralds Seite kann nicht wirklich viel aufhalten. Ein Ape wird von Lasp Gasp gehandelt und ein Hund gesellt sich zum Kannushi. Weitere Länder wollen nicht für Jörg erscheinen. Macht aber auch nichts, solange er mit [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire) insgesamt vier Züge in Folge was zu tun hat.

[Mortify](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mortify) und dann Ghost-Council lassen das Board dann aber recht einseitig erscheinen. Der einsame verbleibene Affe spielt einsam in seinem kleinen Wald herum, während Bob, Ghost-Council, [Empty-Shrine Kannushi](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Empty-Shrine+Kannushi) und fünf Länder auf der anderen Seite eher beeindrucken. Harald ist noch bei 7 Leben.

Eine [Sacred Foundry](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sacred+Foundry) kommt, aber kommt sie rechtzeitig? Bob revealt [Shining Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shining+Shoal), worauf hin Jörg mit [Lightning Helix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Helix) reagiert. So leitet Harald den Schaden um und der Affe beißt ins Gras. Der nächste Draw-Step von Jörg gibt nichts sinnvolles her, so dass er auch für dieses Spiel die Karten zusammenschiebt.

Anzahl der Länder, die Jörg bisher in diesem Match gespielt hat: 3  
 Anzahl der Kreaturen, die Harald bisher gespielt hat: 5

Harald Stein 2 - 0 Jörg Unfried

### Game 3

![Pacifism](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Pacifism)

Im dritten Spiel versucht Jörg sein Glück noch einmal, obwohl Fortuna ihm bislang nicht hold gewesen ist. Tatsächlich nimmt Harald diesmal seine Karten noch mal auf, um sechs neue Karten zu ziehen.

Kannushi zeigt sich diesmal als sinnvolle Hereinnahme, da Jörg zwei [Watchwolf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Watchwolf) in Folge castet. Der zweite Kannushi kommt, Last Gast handelt einen Wolf, und so kann eine kleiner Kleriker sogar für eins hauen geht. Mehrere Züge und Removal-Sprüche später sind die beiden sogar allein auf der Spielfläche und nehmen Jörg ganz schön ran.

Ein elementarer Blocker in Form eines Tin-Street-Hooligan kommt für Jörg ins Spiel. Harald hat das [Last Gasp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Last+Gasp), aber [Giant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Growth) rettet ihn und damit Jörg für eine kleine Weile. Mit [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire) auf den zweiten Kannushi, [Savannah Lions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Savannah+Lions), [Pacifism](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pacifism) auf Haralds Paladin und [Lightning Helix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Helix) auf [Hand of Honor](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hand+of+Honor), ist er plötzlich wieder voll im Geschäft. [Mortify](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mortify) auf [Pacifism](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pacifism) befreit einen Paladin, der sogleich die Red-Zone betritt. Harald, der auf zwei Leben ist, blockt etwas verdutzt mit einem [Watchwolf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Watchwolf), aber [Shining Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shining+Shoal) für Zwei macht klar, weshalb diese Aktion erfolgt ist.

Mit tödlichem Schaden auf dem Stapel gibt Harald auf! Da er sowieso für die Weltmeisterschaft qualifiziert ist, möchte er Jörg diese Gelegenheit nicht verbauen, und das führt zu folgendem Endergebnis:

Harald Stein 2 - 3 Jörg Unfried

  

## Sunday, August 13: 2:13 p.m. - Quarterfinals: Holger Althues ([Enduring Ideal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enduring+Ideal)) vs. Tobias Henke (R/W Burn)

Nach einigen Jahren DM und dementsprechend vielen Top 8 haben wir hier das Vergnügen, zwei Selfmade-Decks im Viertelfinale spielen zu sehen. Tobias Henke spielt ein Rot-Weiß Burn mit 12 One-Drops und [Howling Mine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howling+Mine)s und viel, viel Burn. Holger Althues spielt ein [Enduring Ideal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enduring+Ideal) Deck mit [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) und [Faith's Fetters](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Faith%27s+Fetters), aber ohne [Wrath of God](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wrath+of+God).

### Game 1

Holger gewinnt den Die-Roll und startet. Das Glück hält nicht lange an, denn er entscheidet sich für die Hand mit sechs Karten. Drei Länder, Signet, Leak und [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) müssen langen. Turn one [Scorched Rusalka](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scorched+Rusalka) von Henke "starts the beats". Holger kommt mit Signet, und TobiH hat ein Turn zwei [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire). Das muss dann auch erstmal genug sein, denn nach dem Turn three [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) von Holger heißt es jetzt ohne Länder weitermachen.

Tobias skipt einen Turn und lässt sich im nächsten seinen Isamaru, den er mit der nachgezogenen Foundry bezahlt, [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand)en. Nächste Runde passiert das Ganze noch mal, aber dann klärt der Fast-Hardcounter [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). So, nun ist auch Zeit für [Enduring Ideal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enduring+Ideal) auf [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex), und wieder sitzt Tobias ohne Land da und könnte sich die übrigen Züge eigentlich auch sparen, aber nun ist ja halt ein DM-Top-8-Spiel, das kann man ja mal auskosten.

Holger Althues 1 - 0 Tobias Henke

SB:  
 Tobias boarded nicht.  
 Holger nimmt 4 Research, 4 [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand), 1 Zurs Weirding, 3 [Confiscate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Confiscate) und eine [Form of the Dragon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Form+of+the+Dragon) raus. 4 [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn), 3 [Loxodon Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Loxodon+Hierarch), 1 Fetter's, 1 [Ivory Mask](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ivory+Mask), 2 [Naturalize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Naturalize) und 2 [Jushi Apprentice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jushi+Apprentice) wandern rein.

### Game 2

Tobias startet mit [Battlefield Forge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Battlefield+Forge) und Lion, Holger mit [Brushland](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brushland) und [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn). [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) für Rusalka und [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) gegen [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) auf Holgers Seite beschreiben Turn zwei. Der zweite [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) und eine [Howling Mine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howling+Mine), die [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)ed wird, kommen in Turn drei für Tobi, ein [Simic Signet](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Simic+Signet) bei Holger. Danach geht's direkt weiter mit Isamaru von Henke und [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) auf Foundry von Holger. Aber Holgers [Jushi Apprentice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jushi+Apprentice) wird geshockt und das Bashing geht weiter. Helix wird von dem doch nicht so harten Counter [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) nur verteuert. Holgers übrige vier Leben reichen am Ende nicht aus.

Holger Althues 1 - 1 Tobias Henke

SB:  
 Tobias boarded nun die 4 [Howling Mine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howling+Mine) raus und packt 3 Kami of the Ancient Law und 1 [Plains](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plains) rein.

### Game 3

Althues muss mit dem Land-Signet-Draw einen Mulligan nehmen und hält dann die nächsten sechs. Tobias on the draw mulliganed drei Länder (inklusive Mikokoru), [Shock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shock), Hammer und zwei [Char](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Char), eine Entscheidung, über die man sich streiten kann.

Henke beginnt mit [Sacred Foundry](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sacred+Foundry) und [Isamaru, Hound of Konda](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Isamaru%2C+Hound+of+Konda). Holger macht [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn) mit [Brushland](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brushland) möglich. Kami of the Ancient Law rennt dann auch direkt hinein und Isamaru Nummer zwei versucht sein Glück. Der [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) wird gehammert und Isamaru haut Holger auf 15. [Faith's Fetters](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Faith%27s+Fetters) hält den Hund auf und das Spiel sieht mal wieder so aus als wäre es vorbei. [Savannah Lions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Savannah+Lions) kommt von Henke, Holger klaut mit [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) klaut die [Sacred Foundry](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sacred+Foundry). Holger hat dazu auch Vitu-Ghazi aktiv, [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex)'d die zweite Foundry und lässt einen einsamen [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) bei Henke zurück. Dafür gibt's von ihm noch eine Helix, und es steht 11 (Holger) zu 25 (Tobias). Mikokoru kommt nach, aber der [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) geht durch das nächste [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) in Holgers Besitz, über ein gecastetes [Enduring Ideal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enduring+Ideal). Henke scoopt.

Holger Althues 2 - 1 Tobias Henke

### Game 4

Henke hat Mikokoru als einziges Land und geht auf sechs Handkarten. Holger hat [Adarkar Wastes](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Adarkar+Wastes), [Condemn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Condemn), Leak, Fetters, [Ivory Mask](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ivory+Mask), Apprentice, [Annex](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Annex) und bevorzugt ebenso sechs neue Karten. Fünf Länder und Ideal sind die Belohnung und bleiben.

[Isamaru, Hound of Konda](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Isamaru%2C+Hound+of+Konda) ist das erste Play von Tobi. [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain), Lion, Rusalka, das ist eigentlich schon good game, zumal sich Lion und Rusalka im nächsten Zug verdoppeln. Henke ist ausgetappt und Holger spielt ein verzweifeltes Fetters. Henke schlägt Holger auf 11 und feuert [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) für 3 an Holger's Kopf. [Vitu-Ghazi, the City-Tree](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vitu-Ghazi%2C+the+City-Tree) stellt sich den Massen, der Token-Bau kostet allerdings immer ein Schaden vom [Brushland](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brushland). Henke schwingt mit allen Kreaturen, runter geht's für Holger auf zwei Leben und das war's, da die Rusalka noch für den restlichen Schaden sorgt.

Holger Althues 2 - 2 Tobias Henke

### Game 5

![](https://i.ormos.uk/53/c8/53c8183ff9fa968439552f5b7e86256116b977be34fd107b71aed81954ba2b5f.jpg)*Tobias Henke and Holger Althues on the main table, with the camera transmitting their game for the spectators.*

Fünf Länder und zwei [Jushi Apprentice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jushi+Apprentice) sind nicht das, was man im entscheidenden Spiel haben will, aber Holger hält dennoch. Henke, on the Draw, legt los mit [Sacred Foundry](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sacred+Foundry) und Isamaru. Der Apprentice wird gekontert durch einen Lion und eine Rusalka. Der gute [Court Hussar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Court+Hussar) kommt nach, aber leider bekommt Holger nur drei Länder zur Auswahl. Hammer entsorgt den Hussar, der Lion tauscht mit dem Apprentice und hüpft in die Rusalka. Isamaru haut derweil Holger auf 15, aber dann kommt ein [Faith's Fetters](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Faith%27s+Fetters) auf den Hund, während Henke ausgetappt ist, und Holger geht auf 18 Leben.

Henke macht dann erstmal im nächsten Zug nichts. Der nächste Apprentice kommt und wird von einer [Lightning Helix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Helix) bedroht, die dann aber in einem [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) versinkt. Die zweite Helix schafft es dann aber doch. Der nächste Hussar verspricht Vorteil für Holger, aber wieder ist nur Mana zur Auswahl, diesmal wenigstens in Form von Vitu-Ghazi. Der Hussar dominiert das Board und haut ordentlich zu.

[Shock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shock) und [Char](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Char) burnen Holger end of turn auf 10 life und Tobias spielt in seinem Turn noch [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) hinterher. Nun passiert einen Zug lang nichts auf beiden Seiten, außer das zwei Vitu-Ghazis Token bauen und somit der Druck von Althues mittlerweile auf vier Power gewachsen ist. Land, go von Tobias hilft da nicht weiter.

Flames of the Bloodhand in Holgers Upkeep kann da schon mehr und burnt Holger auf drei. Fünf Token kommen mit Hussar in die Red Zone und Henke geht auf sechs, nachdem die Rusalka blockt und sich opfert, um Althues auf zwei zu bringen. Tobias hat dann das [Char](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Char), um das letzte knappe Spiel für sich zu entscheiden.

Holger Althues 2 - 3 Tobias Henke

  

## Sunday, August 13: 2:21 p.m. - Quarterfinals Maximilian Bracht (Erayo-Ninjas) vs. Carlo Mazzurco (Snakes)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/d1/e8/d1e8c7255023f734b808a2d8326beec4c5c16fad782c08ef90fa21558102cb2f.jpg)*Maximilian Bracht*

Carlo Mazzurco ist derjenige, der Maximilian Bracht das Magicspielen beigebracht hat. Carlo hat Maximilian außerdem einen guten Teil der Karten geliehen, mit denen er heute spielt. Das hindert Maximilian allerdings nicht daran, mit einem guten Gefühl in dieses Match zu gehen: In seiner Vorbereitung hatte Bracht nach eigenen Angaben "nicht mal ein einziges Spiel" gegen Snakes abgegeben.

### Game 1

Carlo gewann den Würfelwurf und startete mit einem Mulligan. Maximilian hielt, hatte aber in den ersten drei Zügen nichts als [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), während Carlo mit [Sakura-Tribe Scout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sakura-Tribe+Scout) ebenfalls sein Mana beschleunigte. [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper) von Carlo löste einen Counter-Krieg aus, mit [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) von Maimilian, [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) von Carlo und [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal) von Maximilian, der mit [Sleight of Hand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sleight+of+Hand) das [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) counterte. Maximilian brachte zudem Carlos Tribe Scout mit [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) auf dessen Hand zurück.

Ein ungeblockter Angriff mit [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) brachte Maximilian einen [Higure, the Still Wind](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Higure%2C+the+Still+Wind), und Carlo stand unter Druck. Bracht counterte seine [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper), hatte aber erstmal keine Antwort auf [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte), die gleich zwei Marken bekam. [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle) von Carlo zeigte [Coat of Arms](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coat+of+Arms), von dem allerdings auch Maximilians zwei Ninjas, Higure und Deep Hours, profitieren würden.

Beide Spieler mussten erstmal denken, und sogar Maximilian Bracht war für eine gute Weile still, während er seine nächsten Spielzüge plante. 17 (Bracht) zu 11 (Mazzurco) standen die Lebenspunkte, und Maximilian musste jetzt Druck machen, um nicht gegen die aktive Jitte und eine Armee von Schlangen immer weiter an Boden zu verlieren. Die Jitte-Marke von Carlo machte den Ninja of Deep Hours fertig - den mit dem Kätzchen-Stempel: Maximilians Ninjas waren nämlich alle Überbleibsel aus diversen Drafts.

Für Carlos zweites [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle) hatte Maximilian [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) und für den zweiten Versuch [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Der Gegenangriff von Maximilian kam ungehindert durch, und Maximilian konnte seinen [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) durch einen erneuten [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) ersetzen. Carlos Jitte entsorgte den Kartenzieher prompt, aber Higure teilte seine drei Schaden aus, setzte Carlos Leben auf acht und suchte einen zweiten Higure. Maximilian war auf Beatdown aus.

"Perfekt", kommentierte Carlo - das haben sich alle hier im Raum von Amiel Tenenbaum abgeguckt - attackierte mit seinem Jitte tragenden [Sakura-Tribe Scout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sakura-Tribe+Scout) und equippte sein ungetapptes [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle). "Diese Jitte ist das allerletzte", kam von Maximilian, dessen blockender Higure trotz [Okina, Temple to the Grandfathers](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Okina%2C+Temple+to+the+Grandfathers) und [Minamo, School at Water's Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Minamo%2C+School+at+Water%27s+Edge) die Jitte-Aktivierung nicht überleben konnte. Carlo konnte Maximilians nächsten [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) mit [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) entsorgen. Ein erster Seshiro von Carlo tauschte mit Maximilians neuem Higure. Der zweite Seshiro traf auf [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) von Maximlilian, aber Carlo hatte [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) und Maximilian schob zusammen.

Carlo Mazzurco 1 - 0 Maximilian Bracht

Sideboarding geschah wie folgt:

Mazzurco:  
 Out: 1 Seshiro, 3 [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand), 3 [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)  
 In: 1 [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte), 2 [Sundering Vitae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sundering+Vitae), 3 [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare), 1 [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle)

Bracht:  
 Out: 4 [Erayo, Soratami Ascendant](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Erayo%2C+Soratami+Ascendant), 1 [Boomerang](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boomerang)  
 In: 4 [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte), 1 [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal)

### Game 2

Maximilan durfte anfangen und eröffnete mit [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter), [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). Carlo versuchte dreimal [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle), das erst beim dritten Mal gegen zwei [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand)s ins Spiel kommen durfte und [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare) zeigte. Maximilian hatte nichts in seinem Zug, und Carlo legte ordentlich vor mit Angriff, [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) und zwei [Sakura-Tribe Scout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sakura-Tribe+Scout). "Ich habe noch nie auch nur ein Spiel gegen Snakes verloren", lamentierte Maximilian und baute sich einen Higure und einen [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter).

Ein Angriff mit allem von Carlo brachte Bracht auf 13, und Carlo legte nach mit [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper). Maximilian lockte das [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare) mit [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte) Nummr eins und legte gleich die zweite hinterher.

Maximilian war allerdings danach ausgetappt und Seshiro oder [Coat of Arms](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coat+of+Arms) von Carlos würden ihn gleich jetzt nahezu umbringen. Carlo griff mal wieder mit allem an: [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper), ihre beiden Töchter, zwei Tribe Scouts, [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle) und [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours). Maximilian blockte den Ninja mit Higure tot, aber Ninjitsu von Carlo machte einen weiteren Ninja. Maximilian ging auf 8 Leben und wehrte Carlos Jitte mit [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal) und [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) ab. Maximilian aktivierte seine eigene Jitte, und ohne Seshiro oder eine eigene Jitte würden Carlos Schlangen bald alle an der spitzen Gabel sterben.

Der nächste Alpha Strike von Carlo zog Maximilian weitere drei Lebenspunkte ab, und gegen [Sosuke's Summons](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sosuke%27s+Summons) und [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper) von Carlo hatte Bracht nichts. End of turn [Boomerang](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boomerang) auf ein Schlangen-Token war nicht sonderlich effektiv, aber nötig, weil Carlo trotzdem noch fünf Token und vier echte Schlangen auf dem Tisch hatte. Angriff mit Higure und der Block von Carlo brachte Brachts Jitte zwei neue Marken, aber Maximilian musste jetzt fehlerlos spielen. Er konzentrierte sich, was seinen sonst so leicht von den Lippen fliegenden trash talk deutlich dämpfte.

Die nächsten beiden Totalangriffe von Carlos brachten Maximilian auf einen mageren Lebenspunkt, der dafür aber fünf Marken auf seiner Jitte sammeln konnte. Damit dezimierte Maximilian Carlos Schlangenarmee und brachte sich auf zwei Leben zurück. Carlo hatte noch fünf Schlangen-Token und sechs Leben. Eine Jitte von oben entsorgte Maximilians Rettungsring. Der brachte als Antwort die meisten von Carlos Schlangen um, und das reichte, dass Carlo zusammen schob.

Carlo Mazzurco 1 - 1 Maximilian Bracht

An diesem Punkt waren alle anderen Viertelfinal-Spiele schon vorbei. Michael Lauter übernahm den Report von jetzt an, weil auch magicthegathering.com nach Updates schrie und ich darum wieder auf Englisch umsteigen musste. Viel Spaß mit den nächsten drei Spielen, zu euch gebracht von Michael Lauter!

### Game 3

Nach einer kurzen Pause geht es weiter. Carlo beginnt, aber Max hat mit Bird, [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) und Doppel-[Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) vier Kreaturen im Spiel, bevor Carlo mit einem [Coiling Oracle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coiling+Oracle) die erste spielt. Es liefert ihm ein Land und ermöglicht damit gleich noch eine Jitte. Ein zweiter Ninja bringt Max mehr Kartenvorteil, ein zweites Oracle von Carlo darf das Spiel nicht betreten (Carlo hätte für den [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) zahlen können, hätte er sein Land für diesen Zug vorher gelegt) und Max kann dann sogar noch einen dritten Ninja präsentieren und ausserdem die Jitte zerstören mit einer eigenen Jitte.

[Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper) bringt Carlo etwas Defensive, aber die Viper fängt sich einen [Boomerang](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boomerang). Beide Token wollen einen Ninja töten und ein [Sundering Vitae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sundering+Vitae) noch einen [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) erledigen, doch Max verhindert beide Pläne mit einem [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) auf ein Token sowie dem vierten Ninja of Deep Hours. Novijen und die erneut ausgespielte Viper ermöglichen es Carlo, beim nächsten Angriff drei Ninjas zu erledigen, aber nachdem ein [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal) verhindert, daß die zuvor von Max gelegte Jitte durch Carlos eigene zerstört wird, gibt Carlo auf, da er auf zwei Leben den nächsten Angriff nicht überlegen kann.

Carlo Mazzurco 1 - 2 Maximilian Bracht

### Game 4

Carlo beginnt Spiel vier, und wieder hat Max die ersten Kreaturen mit Doppel-Thopter und einem Bird, nachdem er [Sosuke's Summons](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sosuke%27s+Summons) von Carlo mit [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) abgewehrt hat. Außerdem hat Max noch eine Jitte, die bei der immensen Stärke seiner Kreaturen (null) gerade wenig hilfreich ist, aber trotzdem von Carlo bei nächster Gelegenheit mit [Sundering Vitae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sundering+Vitae) zerstört wird.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/3b/c2/3bc2d5a36f748d1bb25b7781d8358d9ce94c4d26b14d89e3971f2df6d6bdc596.jpg)*Carlo Mazzurco*

Der Jitte-War geht weiter, als Max eine zweite spielen will, Carlo mit [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare) antwortet und Max seine eigene Jitte remanded. Carlo baut seine Boardposition mit Summons und einem Ninja of Deep Hours aus, während Max immerhin einen [Coat of Arms](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Coat+of+Arms) mit [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) kurzzeitig zurückhalten kann. Zwei Summons später gibt Max auf, nachdem sein Deck in diesem Spiel ihm wirklich nicht viel geliefert hat.

Carlo Mazzurco 2 - 2 Maximilian Bracht

### Game 5

Diesmal eröffnen beide mit one-drops, Max mit Bird und Carlo mit [Sakura-Tribe Scout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sakura-Tribe+Scout). Max repealed den Scout, hat aber dennoch kein drittes Land und legt Erayo. Ein Oracle liefert Carlo ein zweites Land, Max unternimmt dann einen Versuch, Erayo mit [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) und [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) auf eben diesen zu flippen, aber Carlo hat ein [Spell Snare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Snare) als Antwort auf das [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand).

Das zweite Oracle liefert Carlo wieder ein Land und ermöglicht ihm, gleich noch einen Tribe Elder zu legen. Aber nach einer Jitte von Max verschlechtert sich Carlos Situation, da Max mit Hilfe von Erayo nun beginnen kann, Schlangen zu töten. Eine frische [Patagia Viper](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Patagia+Viper) überlebt dann auch nicht lange, und ein Higure verbessert Max' Position weiter.

Doch dann hat Carlo einen sehr guten Zug mit einer [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle) und einem [Chord of Calling](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chord+of+Calling) für Seshiro, nachdem Max versucht hat, mit der Jitte die Oracles und einen Scout zu erledigen. Max meint, dass er nun nicht mehr gewinnen könnte, aber Carlo gibt auf und bringt ihn so ins Viertelfinale, da beide sich sehr gut kennen und Carlo wahrscheinlich ohnehin nicht bei den Worlds antreten könnte.

Carlo Mazzurco 2 - 3 Maximilian Bracht

  

## Sunday, August 13: 4:00 p.m. - The semifinals summary, also in a language you can understand

![](https://i.ormos.uk/44/f1/44f15e122ee832645d9221862ae2c908d58dfd38ce808637b91bc09ac7379e05.jpg)*Is this the right play? Or not? Jörg Unfried is not sure.*

Now that the four matches had narrowed down to two, I still wasn't able to halve myself. So here's what went on in the semifinals. I watched the matches while writing the quarterfinals summaries from unedited feature matches in German which I simultaneously had to bring up to magicthegathering.com standard. Thinking in both languages at the same time is quite taxing for the brain, but I'm trying not to let it turn my brain cells into mush. Here's what went on in the semis:

**Semifinal 1**

Two of the most visible personalities of the German Magic scene met in the first semifinal. If you follow tournament Magic, you are probably aware of Maximilian Bracht, Germany's young rising star. Tobias Henke is also very recognizable, if only by his dreadlocks, and he is also a very vocal voice in the community. Both are great trash talkers, often slinging jokes back and forth and assuming the role of motormouth.

Tobias' burn deck is supposed to have a very good matchup against Bracht's Erayo-Ninja. Bracht had announced yesterday evening at the players party that if he had to play against Henke, he'd certainly lose. Well, he was wrong: Maximilian defeated Tobias in four games. Game 1 was dominated by Tobias' burn. Maximilian managed to flip Erayo, but too late, and [Lava Spike](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lava+Spike) plus Flames of the Bloodhand brought Game 1 to Henke, just as expected. Maximilian brought [Threads of Disloyalty](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Threads+of+Disloyalty) and [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte) in from his sideboard, and Tobias removed his [Howling Mine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howling+Mine)s for [Kami of Ancient Law](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kami+of+Ancient+Law) and [Faith's Fetters](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Faith%27s+Fetters).

Max took game 2 with just those [Threads of Disloyalty](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Threads+of+Disloyalty) and [Umezawa's Jitte](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Umezawa%27s+Jitte), where Henke had no chance. For game 3, Tobias sided out all of his creatures to play around the Threads, but that left him short on stops for Bracht's Ninjas. Helped by a mulligan from Tobias, Maximilian took Game 3 fast. For game 4, Tobias decided that he indeed needed his creatures and brought them back in. It didn't matter anyway because Tobias mulliganed down to three cards in hand and conceded on the spot, disgusted by his draws.

Maximilian Bracht 3 - 1 Tobias Henke

![](https://i.ormos.uk/dd/75/dd7598e0e68c6f0d8ddfd148a82474606df46b38602284f9924b9dd396fbb25c.jpg)*All matches tied at this point. The signs were there to appraise the spectators at the screen about current events.*

**Semifinal 2**

Jörg Unfried with his Zoo deck faced off against Frenchman Amiel Tenenbaum with U/R Vore and lost in four games. The races were mostly close, because Jörg's deck had the burn that gave him enough reach after the first creature *Onslaught* would be halted by a [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore). Game 1 with a double mulligan for Jörg presented Amiel with the chance to wipe out Jörg's team with double [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm), and that's just what the Frenchman did. An 8/8 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) finished the job. [Savannah Lions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Savannah+Lions) and double [Kird Ape](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kird+Ape) were a magnificient start for Jörg in game 2. Supplemented with Helix and [Giant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Growth), the critters were enough to overrun a tiny 3/3 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore).

Amiel struck back in game 3 with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) and [Volcanic Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Hammer) to stunt Jörg's offense, who had mulliganed to boot. When Amiel reset the game with a [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire), had another [Wildfire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wildfire) at the next opportunity and made [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore), Jörg lost. Game 4 started off with a double mulligan for Jörg and early land screw for Amiel. Double [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) saved Amiel from death by [Savannah Lions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Savannah+Lions), and on six life he played a 6/6 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore). Jörg, on 12, only had a shockland for red mana. That extra two damage enabled Amiel to take game 4 just before Jörg had enough burn to even the score.

Amiel Tenenbaum 3 - 1 Jörg Unfried

  

## Sunday, August 13: 4:45 p.m. - Finals: Maximilian Bracht (Erayo-Ninja) vs Amiel Tenenbaum (Vore)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/d9/c3/d9c31ec579ae5c061280e9f614a382e3cd485259aa59c8207f9d756b11846d2b.jpg)*Amiel Tenenbaum sat on his chair, on the edge to find any edge for a wedge into Bracht's plans*

The pre-match banter between the Frenchman Amiel and the Bavarian Maximilian was mostly about France and Germany. Amiel originally comes from France, but lives in Berlin since last year's October. That enabled him to play in the German Nationals, and he now had a chance at the title of German champion. Maximilian commented: "It's all about French-German Friendship, right?" Amiel's snappy answer: "Mitterand is dead." Amiel also took the pen Maximilian was spinning around: "Can I have your pen? Ha, now you have no pen! No pen for Max, so I cannot lose!" But Max Bracht had another one, naturally. Was that an omen?

### Game 1

Max started on the play with a mulligan, and Amiel was happy about that and about his hand. Max still had a second-turn Erayo, which Amiel - "perfect!" - removed with a [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) before it could flip. [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) from Amiel met [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), another [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) met [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand). Max had [Sleight of Hand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sleight+of+Hand), and Amiel asked: "What did you topdeck? I didn't look at your decklist, you can tell me!"

[Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish) from Amiel met yet another [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), and Bracht attacked with [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) and ninjitsu'd [Higure, the Still Wind](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Higure%2C+the+Still+Wind) into play, finding [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours). Both player's draws were pretty solid, as Amiel demonstrated when he bounced Higure with [Eye of Nowhere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Nowhere) and played a 6/6 [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) on the same turn, taking Max to 14.

The German had an answer of sorts, playing Erayo, [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter), [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) on the 'Thopter, play it again, flip Erayo. Amiel's [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) took Max to 8 on the next attack, and the German would need another [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) pretty soon to get rid of the hasty, growing beast. Even if Amiel played his sorceries into the Erayo's Essence, the 'Vore would still grow.

Maximilian swung in with [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) and ninja'd out Higure. Bouncing the [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) repeatedly would also give Max a number of chump blockers, as well as Higure who found [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours). The German went to two from Amiel's next attack. Snapping back with Higure and [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter), he ninja'd out the previously searched [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) and brought Amiel to seven life. Deciding if to search for another Higure or Deep Hours took Max a while but in the end he took [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours).

Minamo gave Max a way to untap his Higure and Max also bounced Amiel's [Boseiju, Who Shelters All](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boseiju%2C+Who+Shelters+All). That removed Amiel's workaround for the Erayo's Essence for a little while. Higure chump-blocked the [Magnivore](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magnivore) and Max's counterattack took Amiel to 5. The German was still on 2 life with [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) and [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours) in play. Amiel left his currently useless Vore on defense.

Max tried [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) on Amiel's Vore, who let it through without even trying to [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) it with the soft counter he had in hand. Max attacked for the win with a [Ninja of the Deep Hours](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ninja+of+the+Deep+Hours), [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) and [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter), with the last two becoming Ninjas, too. Erayo's Essence prevented the [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) in Amiel's hand from doing anything against that.

Amiel Tenenbaum 0 - 1 Maximilian Bracht

Maximilian took out a [Boomerang](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boomerang) for a [Disrupting Shoal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disrupting+Shoal). Amiel sideboarded more extensively, and brought in 2 [Volcanic Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volcanic+Hammer), 2 [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) and 1 [Boseiju, Who Shelters All](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Boseiju%2C+Who+Shelters+All), taking out 2 [Tidings](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tidings), 2 [Demolish](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Demolish) and a [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain).

### Game 2

![](https://i.ormos.uk/37/50/3750ed4dc17364cf71dd13c8c585a0c9238770a7b8f0a7e2e5c2e272f01e32f1.jpg)*Flipping Erayo all weekend, Maximilian Bracht was carried to his victory by Ninjas.*

Amiel kept his opening seven and Bracht checked out the back streets of the city that he will travel to as part of Germany's National team - he took a mulligan. While Amiel shuffled Max's deck, the Bavarian lost control of the pen he was flipping. It bounced on the table, and Amiel took it and threw it behind him with a smile.

Max went first, took two damage from [Breeding Pool](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Breeding+Pool) and played [Sleight of Hand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sleight+of+Hand) off the dual. Max's second turn [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) was [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)ed, with Amiel commenting: "I don't like [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter)." The Frenchman tried to [Stone Rain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Rain) one of Max's [Breeding Pool](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Breeding+Pool)s, but was met with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Another [Ornithopter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721043356/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ornithopter) ninjitsu'd out Higure and Amiel took three damage, with Max searching out yet another Higure. "If you keep that on the board, you will win", said the talkative Frenchman, clearly having the upper hand over Max in the table talk - a rare thing to happen!
